1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a 3d algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 597. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 596/5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 597 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the 3d structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 596. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)
